````math
ensure that the update at each iteration will not change the strategy
too much.

### C.1 Stability of Trajectory Q-value

###### Lemma C.1 (Stability of $`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ under Euclidean regularizer).

Consider when
$`\psi^{\Delta}_{s}(\bm{u})=\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2}\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}u_{a}^{2}`$
for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$ and
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
is satisfied. For any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$ and $`t=1,2,\cdots,T`$, when
$`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ is the trajectory Q-value feedback, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{-}=\frac{6}{\gamma^{2}}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}`$ | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{/}=\frac{6}{\gamma^{2}M_{1}}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}.`$ |  | (C.2) |

###### Proof.

For trajectory Q-value feedback, for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}_{1}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))}-\frac{1}{\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))}\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{\left|\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))-\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))\right|}{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{\left|\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|}{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\left|\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|.`$ |  |

In the last line, we use the fact
$`\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s)),\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))\geq\gamma`$, and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(\sigma(s))}\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\left|\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))-\pi_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\pi_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))\left|\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(\sigma(s))}\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(\sigma(s))}\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))-\pi_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))\right|+\left|\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(\sigma(s))}\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(\sigma(s))}\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|.`$ |  |

We abuse the notion of $`\pi_{1}(\sigma(s))`$ as
$`\pi_{1}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})`$ and
$`\sigma(\sigma(s))=\sigma(s^{\prime})`$, given
$`\sigma(s)=(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})`$.

By recursively applying the process above, we will get

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|\leq\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\left|\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|.`$ |  |

###### Lemma C.2.

Consider the update-rule
([4.2](#S4.E2 "Equation 4.2 ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")).
When we choose $`\psi_{s}^{\Delta}`$ to be negative entropy or Euclidean
distance, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\|\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\|_{1},\left\|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\|_{1}\leq C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\eta_{s},`$ |  | (C.3) |

where

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\frac{2}{\alpha_{s}}\left(2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{\tau\alpha_{s}}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)&\text{Negative Entropy}\\
\frac{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}{\alpha_{s}}\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{2\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}\tau}{M_{1}}&\text{Euclidean Distance.}\end{cases}`$ |  | (C.4) |

The proof is postponed to Appendix
[G.3](#A7.SS3 "G.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").
By using
[Lemma C.2](#A3.Thmtheorem2 "Lemma C.2. ‣ Proof. ‣ Lemma C.1 (Stability of 𝑚^(𝑡)_𝑠 under Euclidean regularizer). ‣ C.1 Stability of Trajectory Q-value ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\left|\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}\Big(\left|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|+\left|\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\left|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|\Big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{3}{\gamma^{2}}\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}\eta_{s^{\prime}}.`$ |  |

At the same time,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left|\frac{m^{(t+1)}_{s}}{m^{(t)}_{s}}-1\right|=\frac{1}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|\leq\frac{3\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma(s)}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}\eta_{s^{\prime}}}{\gamma^{2}M_{1}}.
``` |  |

Therefore,
$`C_{s}^{-}=\frac{6}{\gamma^{2}}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}`$
and
$`C_{s}^{/}=\frac{6\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}}{\gamma^{2}M_{1}}`$
by
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").
∎

###### Lemma C.3 (Stability of $`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ under entropy regularizer).

Consider when
$`\psi^{\Delta}_{s}(\bm{u})=\alpha_{s}\left(\log|\mathcal{A}_{s}|+\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}u_{a}\log u_{a}\right)`$
for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$, and
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[B](#A3.I1.i2 "Condition B ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[C](#A3.I1.i3 "Condition C ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
are satisfied. For any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$ and $`t=1,2,\cdots,T`$,
when $`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ is the trajectory Q-value feedback, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{-}=\frac{12\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)}{\gamma}`$ | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{/}=12\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right).`$ |  | (C.5) |

###### Proof.

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))}-\frac{1}{\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))}\right|=\frac{1}{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))}\left|\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))}{\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))}-1\right|.`$ |  |

We will then use the following lemma which shows the multiplicative
stability when using negative entropy regularizer.

###### Lemma C.4.

When
$`\psi^{\Delta}_{s}(\bm{u})=\alpha_{s}\left(\log|\mathcal{A}_{s}|+\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}u_{a}\log u_{a}\right)`$
for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$,
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[B](#A3.I1.i2 "Condition B ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[C](#A3.I1.i3 "Condition C ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
are satisfied, then for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}},h\in s,t=1,2,\cdots,T`$,
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{1}(h))}{\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma_{1}(h))}-1\right|,\left|\frac{\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}{\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}-1\right|\leq 12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right).`$ |  | (C.6) |

The proof can be found at the end of this section. Then, for any
$`s\in{\mathcal{S}}_{1}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|=\frac{1}{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))}\left|\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))}{\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma(s))}-1\right|\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)}{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma(s))}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)}{\gamma}.`$ |  |

Therefore,
$`C_{s}^{-}=\frac{12\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)}{\gamma}`$.

Similarly, we have
$`C_{s}^{/}=12\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)`$.
∎

###### Proof of Lemma [C.4](#A3.Thmtheorem4 "Lemma C.4. ‣ Proof. ‣ Lemma C.3 (Stability of 𝑚^(𝑡)_𝑠 under entropy regularizer). ‣ C.1 Stability of Trajectory Q-value ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").

Firstly, we invoke Lemma
[C.5](#A3.Thmtheorem5 "Lemma C.5. ‣ Proof of Lemma . ‣ C.1 Stability of Trajectory Q-value ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").

###### Lemma C.5.

Consider update-rule
([4.2](#S4.E2 "Equation 4.2 ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")).
When
$`\psi^{\Delta}_{s}(\bm{u})=\alpha_{s}\left(\log|\mathcal{A}|+\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}u_{a}\log u_{a}\right)`$
for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$, and
[B](#A3.I1.i2 "Condition B ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
is satisfied, for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$, $`a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}`$ and
$`t=1,2,\cdots,T`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\exp\left(-\frac{\eta_{s}}{\alpha_{s}}\left(2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{\tau\alpha_{s}}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\right)\leq\frac{\pi^{(t)}(a{\,|\,}s)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}(a{\,|\,}s)},\frac{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}(a{\,|\,}s)}{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}(a{\,|\,}s)}\leq\exp\left(\frac{\eta_{s}}{\alpha_{s}}\left(2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{\tau\alpha_{s}}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\right)`$ |  | (C.7) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{\text{diff}}=\frac{2}{\alpha_{s}}\left(2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{\tau\alpha_{s}}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right).`$ |  | (C.8) |

The proof is postponed to Appendix
[G.3](#A7.SS3 "G.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").

By Lemma
[C.5](#A3.Thmtheorem5 "Lemma C.5. ‣ Proof of Lemma . ‣ C.1 Stability of Trajectory Q-value ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{1}(h))}{\mu_{1}^{(t)}(\sigma_{1}(h))}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})}{\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})}{\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})}\cdot\frac{\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\pi_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})}{\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{1}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})}\cdot\frac{\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})}{\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\pi_{1}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\exp\left(3\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\frac{\eta_{s^{\prime}}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}\left(2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{\tau\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\right)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\exp\left(3\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{1}(h)}\eta_{s^{\prime}}\right)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\ref{lr:cond1}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\exp\left(6\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\right)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle 1+12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right).`$ |  |

At the last line, we use the fact that $`e^{x}\leq 1+2x`$ for
$`x\in[0,1]`$. Similarly, by using $`1+x\leq e^{x}`$, we can also get
the lower-bound
$`1-6\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)`$.
∎

### C.2 Stability of Q-Value

###### Lemma C.6 (Stability of $`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ under Euclidean regularizer).

Consider when
$`\psi^{\Delta}_{s}(\bm{u})=\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2}\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}u_{a}^{2}`$
for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$, and
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
is satisfied. For any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$ and $`t=1,2,\cdots,T`$, when
$`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ is the Q-value feedback, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{-}=6|s|\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}`$ | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{/}=\frac{6|s|}{M_{1}}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}`$ |  | (C.9) |

where $`|s|`$ is the number of nodes in infoset $`s`$.

###### Proof.

With Q-value feedback, for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}_{1}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)(\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))-\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h)))\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\left|\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))-\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle|s|\max_{h\in s}\left|\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))-\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))\right|.`$ |  |

In the last line, $`|s|`$ denotes the number of nodes in $`s`$. By
similar argument as in Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma C.1 (Stability of 𝑚^(𝑡)_𝑠 under Euclidean regularizer). ‣ C.1 Stability of Trajectory Q-value ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
for any $`h\in s`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))-\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{2}(h)}\pi_{2}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\prod_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{2}(h)}\pi_{2}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{2}(h)}\left|\pi_{2}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\pi_{2}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{2}(h)}\Big(\left|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{2}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|+\left|\pi_{2}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\left|\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu_{2}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})-\pi_{2}^{(t)}(a^{\prime}{\,|\,}s^{\prime})\right|\Big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle 3\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{2}(h)}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}\eta_{s^{\prime}}.`$ |  |

Therefore,
$`\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|\leq 3|s|\max_{h\in s}\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{2}(h)}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}\eta_{s^{\prime}}`$.
Similarly,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\frac{m^{(t+1)}_{s}}{m^{(t)}_{s}}-1\right|=\frac{1}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|\leq\frac{3|s|}{M_{1}}\max_{h\in s}\sum_{(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})\sqsubseteq\sigma_{2}(h)}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}\eta_{s^{\prime}}.`$ |  |

Finally,
$`C_{s}^{-}=6|s|\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}`$
and
$`C_{s}^{/}=\frac{6|s|\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s^{\prime}}^{\rm diff}}{M_{1}}`$
according to
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").
∎

###### Lemma C.7 (Stability of $`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ under Entropy regularizer).

Consider when
$`\psi^{\Delta}_{s}(\bm{u})=\alpha_{s}\left(\log|\mathcal{A}_{s}|+\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}u_{a}\log u_{a}\right)`$
for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$, and
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[B](#A3.I1.i2 "Condition B ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[C](#A3.I1.i3 "Condition C ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
are satisfied. For any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$ and $`t=1,2,\cdots,T`$,
when $`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ is the Q-value feedback, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{-}=12M_{2}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{/}=12\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right).`$ |  | (C.10) |

###### Proof.

With Q-value, for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}_{1}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\frac{m^{(t+1)}_{s}}{m^{(t)}_{s}}-1\right|=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\frac{\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}{\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}-1\right|.`$ |  |

By Lemma
[C.4](#A3.Thmtheorem4 "Lemma C.4. ‣ Proof. ‣ Lemma C.3 (Stability of 𝑚^(𝑡)_𝑠 under entropy regularizer). ‣ C.1 Stability of Trajectory Q-value ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}{\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}\leq 1+12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)`$ |  |

Therefore,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}{\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\left(1+12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\right)\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}{\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle 1+12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right).`$ |  |

Similarly, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{2}^{(t+1)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}{\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{2}^{(t)}(\sigma_{2}(h))}\geq 1-12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right).`$ |  |

Therefore,
$`\left|\frac{m^{(t+1)}_{s}}{m^{(t)}_{s}}-1\right|\leq 12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)`$.
At the same time,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left|m^{(t+1)}_{s}-m^{(t)}_{s}\right|=m^{(t)}_{s}\left|\frac{m^{(t+1)}_{s}}{m^{(t)}_{s}}-1\right|\leq 12\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}M_{2}\max_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{2\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}}{\alpha_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{\tau}{M_{1}}\log\frac{1}{\gamma}\right).\qed
``` |  |

## Appendix D Bidilated Regularizer

Dilated regularizer \[[Hoda et al., 2010](#bib.bib21)\] is the
foundation of previous work \[[Lee et al., 2021](#bib.bib30), [Liu
et al., 2023](#bib.bib31), [Sokota et al., 2023](#bib.bib44)\] to apply
mirror-descent and its variants on sequence-form strategies. Recently,
additional regularization has become a powerful tool for learning in
EFGs \[[Liu et al., 2023](#bib.bib31), [Sokota et al.,
2023](#bib.bib44)\]. Specifically, we can change the objective of the
game to
$`\max_{\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\in\Pi_{1}}\min_{\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}\in\Pi_{2}}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\right)^{\top}\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}})+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$,
where
$`\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}),\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$
is the additional regularizer and $`\tau`$ controls its magnitude. By
adding the additional regularizer, the objective becomes strongly
convex-concave instead of convex-concave, and thus linear convergence
rate can be achieved.

However, the dilated regularizer of player $`i\in[2]`$ is
$`\psi^{\Pi_{i}}(\mu_{i}^{\pi_{i}})=\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}_{i}}\mu_{i}^{\pi_{i}}(\sigma(s))\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{i}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$,
which only counts the reach probability $`\mu_{i}^{\pi_{i}}(\sigma(s))`$
of player $`i`$. Therefore, when sampling a trajectory, to estimate the
additional regularization, importance sampling is needed to offset the
reach probability of player $`3-i`$ and the chance player, which causes
a large dispersion of feedback. Therefore, to avoid importance sampling
on the regularizer, we propose the bidilated regularizer in this
section, to which all players contribute symmetrically. The bidilated
regularizer of player 1 is defined as,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})\coloneqq\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}_{1}}\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}(\sigma(s))\left(\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}(\sigma_{2}(h))\right)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{1}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)).`$ |  | (D.1) |

The additional term is the probability of reaching infoset $`s`$
contributed by player 2 and the chance player. The bidilated regularizer
for player 2 can also be defined similarly. In the following, we will
show that several preferable properties of dilated regularizer still
hold for its bidilated version.

Firstly, the bidilated regularizer
$`\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$ is
still convex with respect to $`\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}`$ and
$`\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}`$ individually. This can be inferred from the fact
that the dilated regularizer is convex with respect to
$`\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}`$ \[[Hoda et al., 2010](#bib.bib21)\]. By enforcing
$`\pi_{2}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\geq\gamma_{s}\bm{\nu}_{s}`$ for every
$`s\in{\mathcal{S}}_{2}`$ with
$`\gamma_{s}>0,\bm{\nu}_{s}\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}`$, where
$`\bm{\nu}_{s}`$ has full support, we have
$`\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}(s,a)\geq\gamma>0`$ for any
$`s\in{\mathcal{S}}_{2},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}`$, where $`\gamma`$ is a
constant. Then, we have the following lemma.

###### Lemma D.1.

For any $`\tau,\gamma>0`$, the Nash equilibrium
$`\bm{\mu}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}=(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})`$
of
[Equation 3.1](#S3.E1 "In 3 Q-Function based Regret Minimization (QFR) ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
is unique.

###### Proof.

Let define
$`F_{1}^{\tau}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})\coloneqq-\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}+\tau\nabla_{\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}}\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})-\tau\nabla_{\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}}\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$
and
$`F_{2}^{\tau}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})\coloneqq\bm{A}^{\top}\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}+\tau\nabla_{\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}}\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})-\tau\nabla_{\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}}\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$.

For any $`\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\in\Pi_{1}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle F_{1}^{\tau}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}),\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}-\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left\langle-\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}+\tau\nabla_{\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}}\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})-\tau\nabla_{\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}}\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}),\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}-\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle-\left\langle\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}-\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle+\tau D_{\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\cdot,\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}).`$ |  |

The last line uses the fact that
$`\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$ is
linear with respect to $`\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}`$.

The counterpart of $`\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}`$ is

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle F_{2}^{\tau}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}),\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}-\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{A}^{\top}\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}+\tau\nabla_{\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}}\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})-\tau\nabla_{\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}}\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}),\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}-\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{A}^{\top}\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}-\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}\right\rangle+\tau D_{\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\cdot)}\left(\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}})+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}).`$ |  |

Let $`\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}=(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$ and
$`F^{\tau}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}})=(F_{1}^{\tau}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}),F_{2}^{\tau}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}))`$.
Then by taking the summation of equations above, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle F^{\tau}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}),\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}-\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}^{\prime}}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle-\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\right)^{\top}\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}+(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}})^{\top}\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}+\tau D_{\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\cdot,\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\right)+\tau D_{\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\cdot)}\left(\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}})-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}).`$ |  |

Then,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle F^{\tau}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}})-F^{\tau}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}^{\prime}}),\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}-\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}^{\prime}}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\tau\Big(D_{\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\cdot,\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\right)+D_{\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\cdot)}\left(\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+D_{\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\cdot,\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}})}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\right)+D_{\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\cdot)}\left(\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\Big).`$ |  |

Since $`\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}},\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}^{\prime}}\succeq\gamma`$,
$`D_{\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\cdot,\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}})}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\right)\geq\gamma\min_{h\in\mathcal{H}}\mu_{c}(h)D_{\psi^{\Pi_{1}}}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\right)`$
by
[Lemma 4.1](#S4.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma 4.1 (Lemma D.2. in ( ) ). ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").
Moreover, there exists $`M>0`$ so that
$`D_{\psi^{\Pi_{1}}}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\right)\geq M\left\|\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}-\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}`$
according to [Hoda et al. \[2010\]](#bib.bib21), [Lee et al.
\[2021\]](#bib.bib30). Therefore, the NE is unique when
$`\tau,\gamma>0`$ by [Rosen \[1965\]](#bib.bib40).∎

## Appendix E Proof of Theorem [4.2](#S4.Thmtheorem2 "Theorem 4.2 (Informal). ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")

###### Theorem E.1 (Formal Version of [Theorem 4.2](#S4.Thmtheorem2 "Theorem 4.2 (Informal). ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")).

Consider the update rule
([4.2](#S4.E2 "Equation 4.2 ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"))
and $`q^{(t)}(s,\cdot)`$ is chosen to be counterfactual value,
trajectory Q-value, or Q-value. When
$`\frac{\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}}{\eta_{s}}\leq\tau C_{s}^{\eta}`$, where
$`C_{s}^{\eta}\coloneqq\frac{\gamma}{2C_{s}^{-}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)`$
for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$ and
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[B](#A3.I1.i2 "Condition B ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[C](#A3.I1.i3 "Condition C ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
are satisfied, we have the following guarantee.

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}D_{\psi^{\Pi}}(\bm{\mu}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\bm{\mu}^{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\pi}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{2}{\gamma\min_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(C_{s}^{/}+C_{s}^{-,Q}\right)\eta_{s}^{\mathrm{anc}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\tau\gamma\min_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}\eta_{s}M_{2}T`$ |  | (E.1) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\tau\gamma\min_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\frac{m^{(1)}_{s}}{\eta_{s}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)),`$ |  |  |

where $`C_{s}^{-,Q}`$ denotes $`C_{s}^{-}`$ associated with Q-value,
regardless of which feedback type $`q^{(t)}(s,\cdot)`$ is.

#### Proof Sketch.

The structure of this section will be as follows. (i). By analyzing the
update-rule
([4.2](#S4.E2 "Equation 4.2 ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")),
we can get the difference of utilities between our strategy
$`\bm{\pi}^{(t)}`$ and an arbitrary strategy $`\bm{\pi}`$ at a single
timestep $`t`$ in each infoset. (ii). By telescoping and using the
smoothness (the strategy as well as the feedback will not change much at
each iteration) of the update-rule, we can further get an upperbound on
the cumulated difference. (iii). By decomposition lemma \[[Liu et al.,
2023](#bib.bib31)\], the difference in each infoset can be extended to
the difference of utility in the whole game. Then, by rearranging the
terms we can get an upperbound on the cumulated distance to the NE.

Firstly, we will use a standard analysis of the update rule
([4.2](#S4.E2 "Equation 4.2 ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")).
For notational simplicity, we define
$`\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\coloneqq\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\mu_{3-p(s)}^{(t)}(\sigma_{3-p(s)}(h))`$.

###### Lemma E.2 (Generalized from Lemma C.2. in [Liu et al. \[2023\]](#bib.bib31)).

Consider the update rule in
([4.2](#S4.E2 "Equation 4.2 ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")).
When $`\psi_{s}^{\Delta}`$ is strongly convex, then for any
$`\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}`$ and
$`t\geq 1`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\begin{split}&\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\\
&+\eta_{s}\tau\left(\frac{\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}-\frac{\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\right)\left(\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\right)\\
&+\eta_{s}\left\langle-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\rangle\\
\leq&D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-(1+\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}})D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\\
&-(1+\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}})D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\\
&-D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))+\eta_{s}\left\langle q^{(t-1)}(s,\cdot)-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\rangle.\end{split}
``` |  |

The proof is postponed to Appendix
[E.2](#A5.SS2 "E.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").

Multiplying $`m^{(t)}_{s}`$ on both sides of Lemma
[E.2](#A5.Thmtheorem2 "Lemma E.2 (Generalized from Lemma C.2. in [ ] ). ‣ Proof Sketch. ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\eta_{s}\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\eta_{s}\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{s}\tau\left(\frac{m^{(t)}_{s}}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)-\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\right)\left(\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{s}m^{(t)}_{s}\langle-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle m^{(t)}_{s}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-(m^{(t)}_{s}+\eta_{s}\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s))D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-(m^{(t)}_{s}+\eta_{s}\tau\frac{m^{(t)}_{s}}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s))D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-m^{(t)}_{s}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))+\eta_{s}m^{(t)}_{s}\left\langle q^{(t-1)}(s,\cdot)-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\rangle.`$ |  |

By noticing the fact that $`\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)`$ is equal to
$`m_{s}^{(t)}`$ associated with Q-value, we can use
[Property 1](#Thmproperty1 "Property 1 (Boundedness). ‣ 4.1 Preliminaries and Basic Properties ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
and
[Property 2](#Thmproperty2 "Property 2 (Stability). ‣ 4.1 Preliminaries and Basic Properties ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
and get,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\frac{m^{(t)}_{s}}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)-\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\right|\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\frac{m^{(t)}_{s}}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}-1\right|\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)+\left|\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)-\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle C_{s}^{/}\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}+C_{s}^{-,Q}\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}.`$ |  |

We also use the fact that $`\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\leq 1`$ in the last
inequality. We use $`C_{s}^{-,Q}`$ to denote the $`C_{s}^{-}`$
associated with Q-value for simplicity.

Furthermore, by using Lemma
[C.2](#A3.Thmtheorem2 "Lemma C.2. ‣ Proof. ‣ Lemma C.1 (Stability of 𝑚^(𝑡)_𝑠 under Euclidean regularizer). ‣ C.1 Stability of Trajectory Q-value ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
and Hölder’s Inequality, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\left\langle q^{(t-1)}(s,\cdot)-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\rangle\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\left\|q^{(t)}(s,\cdot)-q^{(t-1)}(s,\cdot)\right\|_{\infty}\cdot\left\|\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\|_{1}\leq 2C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}\eta_{s}.`$ |  |

where
$`\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}=\max_{t\in[T],s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left\|q^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\right\|_{\infty}`$.

By telescoping and non-negativity of Bregman divergence, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\eta_{s}\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\eta_{s}\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{s}m^{(t)}_{s}\langle-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\rangle\Big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\underbrace{\left(m^{(t)}_{s}-m^{(t-1)}_{s}-\eta_{s}\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)\right)}_{\hbox to7.56pt{\vbox to7.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 3.77788pt\lower-3.77788pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 4.95 0 C 4.95 2.73 2.73 4.95 0 4.95 C -2.73 4.95 -4.95 2.73 -4.95 0 C -4.95 -2.73 -2.73 -4.95 0 -4.95 C 2.73 -4.95 4.95 -2.73 4.95 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.99306pt}{-2.25555pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -2.76 -3.12)} \pgfsys@hbox{61}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\left(C_{s}^{/}+C_{s}^{-,Q}\right)\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}\eta_{s}\tau\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\right|+2C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}\eta_{s}^{2}M_{2}T`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+m^{(1)}_{s}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)).`$ |  |

![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBpZD0iQTUuU1MwLlNTUzAuUHgxLnA3Lm0xLnBpYzEiIGNsYXNzPSJsdHhfcGljdHVyZSBsdHhfbWFya2VkYXNtYXRoIiBoZWlnaHQ9IjEzLjM4IiBvdmVyZmxvdz0idmlzaWJsZSIgc3R5bGU9InZlcnRpY2FsLWFsaWduOi0yLjIzcHgiIHZlcnNpb249IjEuMSIgdmlld2JveD0iMCAwIDEzLjM4IDEzLjM4IiB3aWR0aD0iMTMuMzgiPjxnIHN0eWxlPSItLWx0eC1zdHJva2UtY29sb3I6IzAwMDAwMDstLWx0eC1maWxsLWNvbG9yOiMwMDAwMDA7IiBmaWxsPSIjMDAwMDAwIiBzdHJva2U9IiMwMDAwMDAiIHN0cm9rZS13aWR0aD0iMC40cHQiIHRyYW5zZm9ybT0idHJhbnNsYXRlKDAsMTMuMzgpIG1hdHJpeCgxIDAgMCAtMSAwIDApIHRyYW5zbGF0ZSg2LjY5LDApIHRyYW5zbGF0ZSgwLDYuNjkpIj48cGF0aCBzdHlsZT0iZmlsbDpub25lIiBkPSJNIDYuNDIgMCBDIDYuNDIgMy41NCAzLjU0IDYuNDIgMCA2LjQyIEMgLTMuNTQgNi40MiAtNi40MiAzLjU0IC02LjQyIDAgQyAtNi40MiAtMy41NCAtMy41NCAtNi40MiAwIC02LjQyIEMgMy41NCAtNi40MiA2LjQyIC0zLjU0IDYuNDIgMCBaIE0gMCAwIiAvPjxnIHN0eWxlPSItLWx0eC1zdHJva2UtY29sb3I6IzAwMDAwMDstLWx0eC1maWxsLWNvbG9yOiMwMDAwMDA7IiBmaWxsPSIjMDAwMDAwIiBzdHJva2U9IiMwMDAwMDAiIHRyYW5zZm9ybT0ibWF0cml4KDEuMCAwLjAgMC4wIDEuMCAtMy40NiAtNC40NikiPjxmb3JlaWdub2JqZWN0IHN0eWxlPSItLWx0eC1mby13aWR0aDowLjVlbTstLWx0eC1mby1oZWlnaHQ6MC42NGVtOy0tbHR4LWZvLWRlcHRoOjBlbTtmb250LXNpemU6MTBwdDsiIGhlaWdodD0iOC45MiIgb3ZlcmZsb3c9InZpc2libGUiIHRyYW5zZm9ybT0ibWF0cml4KDEgMCAwIC0xIDAgOC45MikiIHdpZHRoPSI2LjkyIj48c3BhbiBjbGFzcz0ibHR4X2ZvcmVpZ25vYmplY3RfY29udGFpbmVyIj48c3BhbiBjbGFzcz0ibHR4X2ZvcmVpZ25vYmplY3RfY29udGVudCI+PHNwYW4gaWQ9IkE1LlNTMC5TU1MwLlB4MS5wNy5tMS5waWMxLjEiIGNsYXNzPSJsdHhfdGV4dCI+MTwvc3Bhbj48L3NwYW4+PC9zcGFuPjwvZm9yZWlnbm9iamVjdD48L2c+PC9nPjwvc3ZnPg==)
can be upper-bounded by
$`C_{s}^{-}\eta_{s}^{\mathrm{anc}}-\eta_{s}\tau\gamma\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)\leq-\frac{\eta_{s}\tau\gamma}{2}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)`$
by
[Property 2](#Thmproperty2 "Property 2 (Stability). ‣ 4.1 Preliminaries and Basic Properties ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
and letting
$`\frac{\eta_{s}^{\mathrm{anc}}}{\eta_{s}}\leq\frac{\tau\gamma}{2C_{s}^{-}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)`$.
By non-negativity of Bregman divergence, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\eta_{s}\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\eta_{s}\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{s}m^{(t)}_{s}\langle-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\rangle\Big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle-\frac{\eta_{s}\tau\gamma\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}{2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\left(C_{s}^{/}+C_{s}^{-,Q}\right)\eta_{s}\tau\eta_{s}^{\mathrm{anc}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}\eta_{s}^{2}M_{2}T+m^{(1)}_{s}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)).`$ |  |

Then, we will use the following regret decomposition lemma to extend the
difference within an infoset above to the difference of the game.

###### Lemma E.3 (Lemma 5.1 in [Liu et al. \[2023\]](#bib.bib31)).

Let $`\Pi\coloneqq\Pi_{1}\times\Pi_{2}`$, the polytope of all valid
sequence-form joint strategies. For any
$`\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\in\Pi_{1},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}\in\Pi_{2}`$, we let
$`\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}=(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})\in\Pi`$ to
denote the joint strategy,
$`\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}})\colon\Pi\to\mathbb{R}=\psi^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}})+\psi^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$,
and
$`F(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}})\coloneqq(-\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}},\bm{A}^{\top}\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}})`$.
For any
$`\bm{\mu}^{(1)},\bm{\mu}^{(2)},\cdots,\bm{\mu}^{(T)},\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}\in\Pi`$
and $`\tau\geq 0`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle G^{(T),\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}})\coloneqq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}(F(\bm{\mu}^{(t)})^{\top}(\bm{\mu}^{(t)}-\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}})+\tau\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{(t)})-\tau\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}))`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{\bm{\pi}}(\sigma(s))G^{(T)}(s;\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  | (E.2) |
|  | $`\displaystyle R^{(T),\Pi}\coloneqq`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{\bm{\mu}^{\widehat{\bm{\pi}}}\in\Pi}G^{(T),\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widehat{\bm{\pi}}})\leq\max_{\bm{\mu}^{\widehat{\bm{\pi}}}\in\Pi}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{\widehat{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))R^{(T)}(s)`$ |  | (E.3) |

where

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle B^{(t)}_{p}(s,a)\coloneqq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s,a)\sqsubseteq s^{\prime}}\frac{\mu^{(t)}_{p}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}{\mu^{(t)}_{p}(s,a)}\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p}^{(t)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s^{\prime}))`$ |  | (E.4) |
|  | $`\displaystyle G^{(T)}(s;\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\coloneqq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\left\langle-\mathsf{CF}_{p(s)}^{(t)}(s,\cdot)+\tau B^{(t)}_{p(s)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\rangle`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\tau\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\tau\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\Big)`$ |  | (E.5) |
|  | $`\displaystyle R^{(T)}(s)\coloneqq`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{\widehat{\pi}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\in\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}}G^{(T)}(s;\widehat{\pi}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)).`$ |  | (E.6) |

By using
[Lemma E.3](#A5.Thmtheorem3 "Lemma E.3 (Lemma 5.1 in [ ] ). ‣ Proof Sketch. ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")⁴⁴
4 It can be easily generalized to bidilated version by absorbing the
reach probability of player $`3-p`$ and the chance player into
$`\psi_{s}^{\Delta}`$ for $`\psi^{\Pi_{p}}_{\rm bi}`$ and player $`p`$.
For $`\psi^{\Pi_{3-p}}_{\rm bi}`$, since it is linear with respect to
$`\mu^{\pi_{p}}_{p}`$, it can be combined into the counterfactual
value., we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+m^{(t)}_{s}\langle-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\rangle\Big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\left(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}-\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)^{\top}\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{(t)}+\left(\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)^{\top}\bm{A}\left(\mu_{2}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\tau\left(\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(t)})-\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{2}^{(t)})-\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(t)})+\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{2}^{(t)})\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\tau\left(\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(t)})-\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})-\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(t)})+\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})\right)\Big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\left(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}-\mu_{1}^{(t)}\right)^{\top}\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}+\left(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}\right)^{\top}\bm{A}\left(\mu_{2}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\tau\left(\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})-\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})+\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})-\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{(t)},\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\tau\left(\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{2}^{(t)})-\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{2}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})+\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*})-\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\mu_{2}^{(t)})\right)\Big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle 0.`$ |  |

The last inequality is because $`\bm{\mu}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}`$ is the NE
of
$`\max_{\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\in\Pi_{1}\colon\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\succeq\gamma}\min_{\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}\in\Pi_{2}\colon\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}\succeq\gamma}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\right)^{\top}\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}-\tau\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{1}}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})+\tau\psi_{\rm bi}^{\Pi_{2}}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})`$.

Therefore,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 0\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+m^{(t)}_{s}\langle-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\rangle\Big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle-\frac{\tau\gamma\min_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}{2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(C_{s}^{/}+C_{s}^{-,Q}\right)\tau\eta_{s}^{\mathrm{anc}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}\eta_{s}M_{2}T`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\frac{m^{(1)}_{s}}{\eta_{s}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)).`$ |  |

$`(i)`$ is because $`\bm{\mu}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}`$ is the NE of the
regularized and perturbed EFG. Then, by rearranging the terms, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}D_{\psi^{\Pi}}(\bm{\mu}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\bm{\mu}^{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\pi}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}})`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{2}{\gamma\min_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left(C_{s}^{/}+C_{s}^{-,Q}\right)\eta_{s}^{\mathrm{anc}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\tau\gamma\min_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}\eta_{s}M_{2}T`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\tau\gamma\min_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\frac{m^{(1)}_{s}}{\eta_{s}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)).`$ |  |

The first equality is by
[Lemma 4.1](#S4.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma 4.1 (Lemma D.2. in ( ) ). ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").
Now, we achieved best-iterate convergence to the regularized NE
$`\bm{\mu}^{(\tau,\gamma),*}`$ in terms of Bregman divergence.∎

### E.1 Proof of Lemma [4.1](#S4.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma 4.1 (Lemma D.2. in ( ) ). ‣ 4.2 Convergence with Full Information Feedback ‣ 4 Analysis ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")

By definition of Bregman divergence, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle D_{\psi^{\Pi}}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}},\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}})=\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}})-\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}})-\left\langle\nabla\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}),\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}-\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}\right\rangle.`$ |  |

For notational simplicity, we use $`\mu^{\bm{\pi}}(s,a)`$ as
$`\mu^{\bm{\pi}}_{p(s)}(s,a)`$. Since
$`\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}})=\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))\psi_{s}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))}\right)`$,
for any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}`$, the gradient
$`\nabla_{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,a)}\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}})`$
is equal to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,a)}\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}})=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}\colon\sigma(s^{\prime})=(s,a)}\left(\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right)-\left\langle\nabla\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right),\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right\rangle\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\nabla_{a}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))}).`$ |  |

Therefore,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}),\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}\right\rangle`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,a)\sum_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}\colon\sigma(s^{\prime})=(s,a)}\left(\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right)-\left\langle\nabla\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right),\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right\rangle\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,a)\nabla_{a}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}\sum_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}\colon\sigma(s^{\prime})=(s,a)}\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right)`$ |  | (E.7) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}\sum_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}\colon\sigma(s^{\prime})=(s,a)}\left\langle\nabla\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right),\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)\right\rangle`$ |  | (E.8) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,a)\nabla_{a}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))}).`$ |  | (E.9) |

Note that
([E.7](#A5.E7 "Equation E.7 ‣ E.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"))
is equal to
$`\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))\psi_{s}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))}\right)=\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}})`$
due to the uniqueness of $`\sigma(s^{\prime})`$.

Similarly, due to the uniqueness of $`\sigma(s^{\prime})`$,
([E.8](#A5.E8 "Equation E.8 ‣ E.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"))
is equal to
$`-\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left\langle\nabla\psi_{s}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))}\right),\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)\right\rangle`$,
which is equal to the negative of
([E.9](#A5.E9 "Equation E.9 ‣ E.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"))
and thus cancel out. Therefore,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}),\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}\right\rangle=\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}).`$ |  |

Moreover,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\psi^{\Pi}(\bm{\mu}^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}),\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}\sum_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}\colon\sigma(s^{\prime})=(s,a)}\mu^{\bm{\pi}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}\mu^{\bm{\pi}}(s,a)\sum_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}\colon\sigma(s^{\prime})=(s,a)}\left\langle\nabla\psi_{s^{\prime}}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right),\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s^{\prime},\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s^{\prime}))}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}},a\in\mathcal{A}_{s}}\mu^{\bm{\pi}}(s,a)\nabla_{a}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,a)})`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\mu^{\bm{\pi}}(\sigma(s))\left(\psi_{s}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))}\right)+\left\langle\nabla\psi_{s}^{\Delta}\left(\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))}\right),\frac{\mu^{\bm{\pi}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\bm{\pi}}(\sigma(s))}-\frac{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(s,\cdot)}{\mu^{\widetilde{\bm{\pi}}}(\sigma(s))}\right\rangle\right).`$ |  |

Therefore,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle F^{\tau}(\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}),\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}}-\bm{\mu}^{\bm{\pi}^{\prime}}\right\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle-\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\right)^{\top}\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}+(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}})^{\top}\bm{A}\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}+\tau D_{\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\cdot,\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})}\left(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}}\right)+\tau D_{\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\cdot)}\left(\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}})-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{1}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})+\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}^{\prime}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}})-\tau\psi^{\Pi_{2}}_{\rm bi}(\mu_{1}^{\pi_{1}},\mu_{2}^{\pi_{2}^{\prime}}).`$ |  |

### E.2 Proof of Lemma [E.2](#A5.Thmtheorem2 "Lemma E.2 (Generalized from Lemma C.2. in [ ] ). ‣ Proof Sketch. ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")

Firstly, we introduce the following lemma.

###### Lemma E.4.

Let $`\mathcal{C}`$ be a convex set and
$`\bm{x}^{(1)}=\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{C}}\left\{\left\langle\bm{g},\bm{x}\right\rangle+\tau_{0}\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x})+\frac{1}{\eta}D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x},\bm{x}^{(0)})\right\}`$,
where $`\psi^{\mathcal{C}}`$ is a strongly-convex function in
$`\mathcal{C}`$ and $`\tau_{0}\geq 0`$ is a constant. Then, for any
$`\bm{x}^{(2)}\in\mathcal{C}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\eta\tau_{0}\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(1)})-\eta\tau_{0}\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(2)})+\eta\left\langle\bm{g},\bm{x}^{(1)}-\bm{x}^{(2)}\right\rangle`$ |  | (E.10) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(2)},\bm{x}^{(0)})-(1+\eta\tau_{0})D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(2)},\bm{x}^{(1)})-D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(1)},\bm{x}^{(0)}).`$ |  |  |

The proof is postponed to the end of this section.

Plug
$`\bm{x}^{(0)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\bm{x}^{(1)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\bm{x}^{(2)}=\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\bm{g}=-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\psi^{\mathcal{C}}=\psi_{s}^{\Delta},\eta=\eta_{s},\tau_{0}=\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}`$
into
Lemma [E.4](#A5.Thmtheorem4 "Lemma E.4. ‣ E.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
with
$`\mathcal{C}=\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}_{\gamma_{s},\bm{\nu}_{s}}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{s}\langle\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot)\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\left(1+\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\right)D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)).`$ |  |

Plug
$`\bm{x}^{(0)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\bm{x}^{(1)}=\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\bm{x}^{(2)}=\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\bm{g}=-q^{(t-1)}(s,\cdot),\psi^{\mathcal{C}}=\psi_{s}^{\Delta},\eta=\eta_{s},\tau_{0}=\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}`$
into
Lemma [E.4](#A5.Thmtheorem4 "Lemma E.4. ‣ E.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
with
$`\mathcal{C}=\Delta^{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}_{\gamma_{s},\bm{\nu}_{s}}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{s}\langle\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),-q^{(t-1)}(s,\cdot)\rangle`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-(1+\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}})D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)).`$ |  |

Summing them up and adding
$`\eta_{s}\left\langle q^{(t-1)}(s,\cdot)-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\rangle`$
to both sides, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}}\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  | (E.11) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{s}\tau(\frac{\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}}-\frac{\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}})(\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-\psi_{s}^{\Delta}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{s}\langle-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\rangle`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))-(1+\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t)}(s)}{m^{(t)}_{s}})D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-(1+\eta_{s}\frac{\tau\mu_{-p(s)}^{(t-1)}(s)}{m^{(t-1)}_{s}})D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s),\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s))+\eta_{s}\left\langle q^{(t-1)}(s,\cdot)-q^{(t)}(s,\cdot),\pi^{(t)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)-\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\pi\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t+1)}_{p(s)}(\cdot{\,|\,}s)\right\rangle.`$ |  | ∎ |

###### Proof of Lemma [E.4](#A5.Thmtheorem4 "Lemma E.4. ‣ E.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(2)},\bm{x}^{(0)})-(1+\eta\tau_{0})D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(2)},\bm{x}^{(1)})-D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(1)},\bm{x}^{(0)})`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left(\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(2)})-\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(0)})-\left\langle\nabla\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(0)}),\bm{x}^{(2)}-\bm{x}^{(0)}\right\rangle\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-(1+\eta\tau_{0})\left(\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(2)})-\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(1)})-\left\langle\nabla\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(1)}),\bm{x}^{(2)}-\bm{x}^{(1)}\right\rangle\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\left(\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(1)})-\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(0)})-\left\langle\nabla\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(0)}),\bm{x}^{(1)}-\bm{x}^{(0)}\right\rangle\right)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\eta\tau_{0}\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(1)})-\eta\tau_{0}\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(2)})+\left\langle(1+\eta\tau_{0})\nabla\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(1)})-\nabla\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(0)}),\bm{x}^{(2)}-\bm{x}^{(1)}\right\rangle.`$ |  |

Since

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\bm{x}^{(1)}=\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{C}}\left\{\left\langle\bm{g},\bm{x}\right\rangle+\tau_{0}\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x})+\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x})-\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(0)})-\left\langle\nabla\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(0)}),\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{(0)}\right\rangle\right)\right\},
``` |  |

by first-order optimality, we have,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left\langle\eta\bm{g}+(1+\eta\tau_{0})\nabla\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(1)})-\nabla\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(0)}),\bm{x}^{(2)}-\bm{x}^{(1)}\right\rangle\geq 0.`$ |  |

Therefore,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(2)},\bm{x}^{(0)})-(1+\eta\tau_{0})D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(2)},\bm{x}^{(1)})-D_{\psi^{\mathcal{C}}}(\bm{x}^{(1)},\bm{x}^{(0)})`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\eta\tau_{0}\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(1)})-\eta\tau_{0}\psi^{\mathcal{C}}(\bm{x}^{(2)})+\eta\left\langle\bm{g},\bm{x}^{(1)}-\bm{x}^{(2)}\right\rangle.\qed`$ |  |

## Appendix F Proof of Theorem [3.1](#S3.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem 3.1 (Informal). ‣ 3 Q-Function based Regret Minimization (QFR) ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")

###### Theorem F.1 (Formal Version of [Theorem 3.1](#S3.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem 3.1 (Informal). ‣ 3 Q-Function based Regret Minimization (QFR) ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")).

Consider
[Algorithm 1](#alg1 "In 3 Q-Function based Regret Minimization (QFR) ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence").
When $`\frac{\eta_{s}^{\rm anc}}{\eta_{s}}\leq\tau C_{s}^{\eta,T}`$ for
any $`s\in{\mathcal{S}}`$, where
$`C_{s}^{\eta,T}\coloneqq\frac{\gamma^{2}\sum_{h\in s}\mu_{c}(h)}{2C_{s}^{-}\left(\log T+\log|{\mathcal{S}}|+\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)}`$,
and
[A](#A3.I1.i1 "Condition A ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[B](#A3.I1.i2 "Condition B ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence"),
[C](#A3.I1.i3 "Condition C ‣ Appendix C Stability of Trajectory Q-value and Q-Value ‣ A Policy-Gradient Approach to Solving Imperfect-Information Games with Best-Iterate Convergence")
are satisfied, we have the following guarantee with probability
$`1-2\delta`$.

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}D_{\psi^{\Pi}}(\bm{\mu}^{(\tau,\gamma),*},\bm{\mu}^{\mkern 1.5mu\overline{\mkern-1.5mu\bm{\pi}\mkern-1.5mu}\mkern 1.5mu^{(t)}})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{4C_{\rm visit}}{\tau}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}C_{s}^{\mathrm{diff}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}\eta_{s}M_{2}T`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{2C_{\rm visit}}{\tau}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\frac{1}{\eta_{s}\mu_{p(s)}^{(t_{s}^{1})}(\sigma(s))}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\max_{\bm{x},\bm{y}\in\Delta_{\gamma_{s},\bm{\nu}_{s}}^{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\bm{x},\bm{y})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{4C_{\rm visit}^{2}}{\tau}\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\frac{1}{\eta_{s}}\mu^{(\tau,\gamma),*}(\sigma(s))\max_{\bm{x},\bm{y}\in\Delta_{\gamma_{s},\bm{\nu}_{s}}^{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\bm{x},\bm{y})`$ |  | (F.1) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{4C_{\rm visit}}{\tau}\left(1+\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\left(\left\|\bm{q}\right\|_{\infty}+\tau\psi^{\max}\right)\sqrt{2T\log\frac{|{\mathcal{S}}|}{\delta}}+\sum_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\frac{4C_{\rm visit}^{2}}{\eta_{s}\tau}\max_{\bm{x},\bm{y}\in\Delta_{\gamma_{s},\bm{\nu}_{s}}^{|\mathcal{A}_{s}|}}D_{\psi_{s}^{\Delta}}(\bm{x},\bm{y}),`$ |  |  |

where
````
